# Mexican Spanish 06 (C2 and advanced-use bridge) coverage matrix

## Purpose and coverage-led sizing

This stage develops distinct advanced-use tasks in educated contemporary central Mexican Spanish. The current proposal contains 1,310 cards in 50 independently authored and reviewed slices: 25 cards in S01–S46, and 40 in S47–S50.

Coverage determines the accepted count. The initial planning range is not a quota, and this proposal is below it. Each slice states its required advance beyond actual C1 coverage. Final focused reviews must check all twelve areas for unexplained gaps; numerical completion cannot establish coverage or justify filler. This audio-cloze bridge does not certify C2 proficiency.

S01–S03 and S14 each retired fifteen authored candidates before acceptance: sixty in total. Their overlapping tasks, inherited distinctions, unsupported inferences, and unnecessary target expansions were not replaced merely to restore the planned count. No accepted card was removed.

Separately, unauthored counts and blocks were narrowed where C1 or accepted Stage 06 already supplies the task. C1 0756–0779 covers nominalization and reduced-clause operations; C1 1400–1519 covers refusal, bounded figurative stance and ordinary implication repair; C1 1675–1714 and accepted S04 cover linked consent and constraint revision. These remain prerequisites and supporting resources. New reference, pragmatics and mediation slices must instead perform the consequential viewpoint, attribution or interaction operation specified below. Removed planning slots are not retired authored candidates.

The unauthored professional blocks were also narrowed after comparison with C1 meeting, interview, feedback and correspondence cards. Ordinary premise rejection, bounded revisions and feedback remain prerequisites. S37–S41 now require the relayed-challenge, record-circulation, nonmatching-version, transformation-responsibility and evidence/authority interactions specified below. These are proposals whose actual advance must be verified against earlier accepted Stage 06 before authoring and in the final focused reviews.

The unauthored parenthetical block was consolidated into accepted S22, whose cards 0526, 0531, 0535, 0536, 0547 and 0548 already restore a suspended viewpoint after commentary, a listener's hypothetical, a qualification or a deictic interruption. S24 supplies selective reference resumption; ordinary parenthetical and attachment work remains inherited support. Twenty-five unauthored slots were removed, with no accepted source or ledger changes. Later unaccepted slice labels and ranges were synchronized.

The five unauthored regional outlines were consolidated into one mixed recognition slice. Their reference, event-identity, force and mediation operations already have earlier prerequisites; the additional regional input does not justify five separate inventories. S46 retains all four required kinds of regional difference in a limited set of contextualized encounters, with Mexican production throughout. One hundred unauthored slots were removed, with no accepted source or ledger changes. The regional focused review must verify this breadth rather than infer it from the count.

The unauthored aggregate-versus-subgroup block and repeated parenthetical-focus block were also removed. Accepted S01 already treats opposing aggregate/category movements, composition effects and linked uneven access (0001, 0006–0008); S22 and S25 already restore viewpoint and focused scope across commentary or regrouping (0526, 0535–0536, 0547–0548, 0601, 0613, 0621–0625). Those uses remain available in public and integrated tasks rather than receiving another forty-instance block each. Eighty additional unauthored slots were removed; accepted cards and ledger rows are unchanged.

The four remaining unauthored public/cultural outlines were narrowed to twenty-five cards each after comparison with actual C1 framing, cultural interpretation, technology and participation cards. S42–S45 now propose competing-frame evaluation, disagreement across represented voices, public representation of user acts and contested consultation. Their actual advance must be checked against accepted earlier Stage06 before authoring and in the public focused review; the revised descriptions alone do not prove progression. Sixty unauthored slots were removed, without changing accepted content.

The unauthored lexical-evidence and idiomatic-ownership outlines were consolidated into distributed practice rather than repeated as two forty-card blocks. Actual C1 2110, 2116, 2120, 2122, 2130 and 2143 already encode evidential distinctions through lexical choice; 2231 and 2242 preserve adopted or reported stance. Accepted Stage 06 0001, 0010, 0012, 0131 and 0149 add interacting evidential or category limits, while 0301, 0303, 0312, 0320, 0324, 0325 and S26 use stance and figurative wording with consequential source ownership. These remain explicit material for the lexical/integrated focused review, which must identify any unexplained gap. Eighty unauthored slots were removed; no accepted card or ledger row changed. The four remaining integrated outlines require comparison with actual later accepted Stage 06 before authoring, not acceptance based on their labels.

Before integrated authoring, comparison with actual C1 2223–2262 and accepted Stage 06 linked-commitment, mediation, transformation, public-response and regional-recognition cards establishes those operations as prerequisites, not new lexical tasks. S47–S50 must make their combination consequential for a personal decision, practical repair, public response or audience transformation. A second source, added reservation, new setting or longer cue does not by itself supply that interaction. Their proposed counts remain subject to independent review; the descriptions below do not establish acceptance.

These are proposed coverage ranges, not a requirement to preserve overlapping unaccepted candidates. Reviews may retire redundant candidates and synchronize later unaccepted ranges before acceptance. Earlier accepted ranges, actual targets, and direct burdens are recorded in the ledger below. Regional recognition remains limited.

## Immutable prerequisites and progression

Mexican Spanish 01–05 provide 7,462 accepted cards. Stage 05 already teaches controlled viewpoint, mood, evidence limits, short-source synthesis, qualified argument, reference, mediation, and register adaptation. Stage 06 advances through interacting scopes, nested attribution, unequal knowledge, consequential reservations, selective revision, and controlled transformations—not harder synonyms, longer passages, or new labels alone. Each slice below states the required advance. The accepted ledger records the actual target and direct learning burden of each accepted card.

All earlier Spanish and all Japanese, Korean, and French work remain unchanged. Each slice requires Mexican Spanish 01–05 and accepted earlier Stage 06 slices only. Previously learned grammar may support a new discourse task. A new same-slice or later-slice construction or communicative use may appear only as the explicitly introduced current target; it cannot be assumed as incidental support.

## Binding card, source, and originality rules

- Use a complete natural sentence or short coherent passage/exchange. Put all evidence needed for interpretation in the cue; no unseen source or unexplained cultural knowledge.
- Every literal target occurs exactly once and equals the answer. The target itself realizes the assigned burden; a construction elsewhere in the cue does not justify an unrelated detail target.
- For a finite clause, ordinarily hide only the assigned inflected form, necessary clitic plus form, required compound form, or genuinely indivisible reusable collocation. Keep triggers, licensing meaning, factuality, subjects, objects, complements, adverbs, and time relations visible.
- Do not hide complete or near-complete propositions, except compact reusable fixed expressions. Keep useful visible context. No connector-plus-predicate fragments, mechanical shortening, relative padding, or arbitrary enlargement for uniqueness.
- Keep the target within one sentence, quotation, paragraph, and speaker turn; terminal punctuation stays outside it.
- Primary targets and complete cues are unique across Spanish 01–06 under NFC, Unicode case-folding, and collapsed whitespace. Distinct strings alone do not prove distinct tasks. Do not repair duplication by altering earlier stages or appending arbitrary names, nouns, dates, places, numbers, modifiers, or complements.
- Vary syntax, domains, relationships, evidence patterns, and communicative acts. No dictionary, idiom catalog, tense/number/gender/relative/connector table, document-field inventory, or template substitution block.
- Use contemporary central Mexican grammar, vocabulary, tense preferences, and stable tú/usted relationships; plural address uses ustedes. Standard accents, inverted punctuation, and canonical NFC spelling are required.
- Non-Mexican forms are permitted only as explicitly framed, bounded recognition sources, never productive targets or a second voice. Do not generalize an individual's pragmatic intention into a universal regional fact.
- Lemma, concise English meaning, and genuine accepted alternatives describe the actual span. Omit unused optional fields, reading, IPA, respelling, and internal process language.
- Original source statements may disagree. Preserve agency, qualification, factuality, and evidence status. Do not fabricate real-person or real-institution findings, quotations, or authority.
- Health/access situations are communication and access tasks only; no diagnosis, treatment, legal, financial, technical-safety, or other professional advice.

## Advanced-use and phase boundaries

No productive future subjunctive, rare historical forms, artificial archaism, opaque allusion, unrestricted regional imitation, specialist writing, long unseen-source examination, or pipeline changes. No recognition-only historical quotation is planned. Complexity must remain usable as a self-contained short cue under the existing runner; do not lengthen cues to manufacture progression.

Phase 1 is source-only. Reference access, speech models, audio, full archives, samples, Phase 2, another stage, final-bundle work, and publication are excluded. Existing mocked/tiny repository tests remain permitted.

## Review allocation

Twelve focused reviews cover: synthesis S01–S06; argument S07–S12; stance/modality S13–S17; narration S18–S22; reference S23–S25; pragmatics S26–S28; mediation S29–S31; audience/register S32–S36; professional communication S37–S41; public/cultural discourse S42–S45; regional recognition S46; lexical precision/integrated delivery S47–S50. Review relevant recycled uses outside the primary ranges as well.

After author/editor acceptance and twelve focused reviews, three consecutive partition audits cover S01–S22, S23–S37, and S38–S50, with their accepted exact ranges synchronized at that point. Review every changed slice. Two independent final audits inspect every accepted card against the same final source: A for scope/fairness/metadata and B for originality/alignment/prerequisites/advance/variety/source fidelity/boundaries. Both must return the complete count and zero remaining findings.

## Slice 01 — Qualified intersections of evidence

- **IDs:** `es-06-0001`–`es-06-0025` (25 accepted cards).
- **Grammar and construction burden:** Produce a focused synthesis that preserves interacting evidential levels or linked conditions; simple part-versus-whole, single-group restrictions, and added time/place details are inherited support, not new tasks.
- **Communicative function:** State only the overlap supported by three unequal source statements.
- **Lexical domains and reusable chunks:** Community observations, service records, access accounts. Select distinct context-licensed forms or indivisible collocations, never an inventory of interchangeable expressions.
- **Pronunciation and orthography burden:** Standard Mexican stress, connected clause grouping, contrastive focus and parenthetical boundaries; canonical NFC accents and punctuation. Text must establish distinctions that prosody alone cannot guarantee.
- **Register and relationship:** Contemporary educated Mexican conversation, neutral explanation, or accessible professional/public communication as the named participants and situation require; preserve relationship-appropriate address.
- **Listening and production targets:** Recover the supplied source, scope, and interaction relations from the complete cue and produce the one focused span that directly performs this slice's act. Keep all disambiguating context visible.
- **Prerequisites:** Mexican Spanish 01–05; no Stage 06 support assumed.
- **Exclusions:** All binding stage-wide scope, originality, and advanced-use boundaries; no unrelated adjunct target, repeated formula expanded with arbitrary content, or same/later-slice use assumed as support.
- **Advance beyond Stage 05:** Require a consequential interaction absent from the C1 analogue, such as opposing aggregate/subgroup movements, composition versus within-group change, or unlinked source properties that do not establish their joint occurrence. Each case must involve a distinct communicative inference, not a new noun, date, evidence verb, or third confirmation.

## Slice 02 — Clarifying decision-critical contradictions

- **IDs:** `es-06-0026`–`es-06-0050` (25 accepted cards).
- **Grammar and construction burden:** Formulate a focused clarification that distinguishes incompatible definitions or observation levels before they are used to justify a shared decision.
- **Communicative function:** Ask what would discriminate between two live interpretations without rewriting either source's account.
- **Lexical domains and reusable chunks:** Housing reports, event evaluations, education accounts. Select distinct context-licensed forms or indivisible collocations, never an inventory of interchangeable expressions.
- **Pronunciation and orthography burden:** Standard Mexican stress, connected clause grouping, contrastive focus and parenthetical boundaries; canonical NFC accents and punctuation. Text must establish distinctions that prosody alone cannot guarantee.
- **Register and relationship:** Contemporary educated Mexican conversation, neutral explanation, or accessible professional/public communication as the named participants and situation require; preserve relationship-appropriate address.
- **Listening and production targets:** Recover the supplied source, scope, and interaction relations from the complete cue and produce the one focused span that directly performs this slice's act. Keep all disambiguating context visible.
- **Prerequisites:** Mexican Spanish 01–05 and accepted Stage 06 S01–S01.
- **Exclusions:** All binding stage-wide scope, originality, and advanced-use boundaries; no unrelated adjunct target, repeated formula expanded with arbitrary content, or same/later-slice use assumed as support.
- **Advance beyond Stage 05:** Beyond C1 reconciliation of different criteria, identify and request the missing distinction that determines which proposed decision follows; do not prematurely resolve the disagreement.

## Slice 03 — Partial corroboration across dependent sources

- **IDs:** `es-06-0051`–`es-06-0075` (25 accepted cards).
- **Grammar and construction burden:** Separate the part of a report independently corroborated by a third witness from the part merely repeated by sources with a shared origin.
- **Communicative function:** Report exactly which claim gains independent support and which still rests on one unverified origin.
- **Lexical domains and reusable chunks:** Local reporting, workplace updates, travel disruptions. Select distinct context-licensed forms or indivisible collocations, never an inventory of interchangeable expressions.
- **Pronunciation and orthography burden:** Standard Mexican stress, connected clause grouping, contrastive focus and parenthetical boundaries; canonical NFC accents and punctuation. Text must establish distinctions that prosody alone cannot guarantee.
- **Register and relationship:** Contemporary educated Mexican conversation, neutral explanation, or accessible professional/public communication as the named participants and situation require; preserve relationship-appropriate address.
- **Listening and production targets:** Recover the supplied source, scope, and interaction relations from the complete cue and produce the one focused span that directly performs this slice's act. Keep all disambiguating context visible.
- **Prerequisites:** Mexican Spanish 01–05 and accepted Stage 06 S01–S02.
- **Exclusions:** All binding stage-wide scope, originality, and advanced-use boundaries; no unrelated adjunct target, repeated formula expanded with arbitrary content, or same/later-slice use assumed as support.
- **Advance beyond Stage 05:** C1 already recognizes shared provenance; the new task apportions corroboration across different components of the same account instead of accepting or rejecting the whole report.

## Slice 04 — Separate agreements without invented collective assent

- **IDs:** `es-06-0076`–`es-06-0100` (25 accepted cards).
- **Grammar and construction burden:** Recover the participant-specific scope and interacting conditions of linked agreements without converting them into an all-party commitment.
- **Communicative function:** Recover the actual limited agreements and clarify what has not been jointly accepted.
- **Lexical domains and reusable chunks:** Neighborhood decisions, collective projects, family planning. Select distinct context-licensed forms or indivisible collocations, never an inventory of interchangeable expressions.
- **Pronunciation and orthography burden:** Standard Mexican stress, connected clause grouping, contrastive focus and parenthetical boundaries; canonical NFC accents and punctuation. Text must establish distinctions that prosody alone cannot guarantee.
- **Register and relationship:** Contemporary educated Mexican conversation, neutral explanation, or accessible professional/public communication as the named participants and situation require; preserve relationship-appropriate address.
- **Listening and production targets:** Recover the supplied source, scope, and interaction relations from the complete cue and produce the one focused span that directly performs this slice's act. Keep all disambiguating context visible.
- **Prerequisites:** Mexican Spanish 01–05 and accepted Stage 06 S01–S03.
- **Exclusions:** All binding stage-wide scope, originality, and advanced-use boundaries; no unrelated adjunct target, repeated formula expanded with arbitrary content, or same/later-slice use assumed as support.
- **Advance beyond Stage 05:** Go beyond C1 permission scope, silence, bundled offers, and stated reservations: preserve which linked commitments can coexist, whose acceptance each depends on, and which proposed collective action has never been jointly accepted.

## Slice 05 — Propagating a correction through linked accounts

- **IDs:** `es-06-0101`–`es-06-0125` (25 accepted cards).
- **Grammar and construction burden:** Propagate a source's specific correction through linked retellings or derived claims, preserving independently supported components and marking where the correction's reach remains unresolved.
- **Communicative function:** Correct the downstream account at the level actually affected without retroactively rewriting what earlier speakers knew or asserted.
- **Lexical domains and reusable chunks:** Meeting summaries, service explanations, project decisions. Select distinct context-licensed forms or indivisible collocations, never an inventory of interchangeable expressions.
- **Pronunciation and orthography burden:** Standard Mexican stress, connected clause grouping, contrastive focus and parenthetical boundaries; canonical NFC accents and punctuation. Text must establish distinctions that prosody alone cannot guarantee.
- **Register and relationship:** Contemporary educated Mexican conversation, neutral explanation, or accessible professional/public communication as the named participants and situation require; preserve relationship-appropriate address.
- **Listening and production targets:** Recover the supplied source, scope, and interaction relations from the complete cue and produce the one focused span that directly performs this slice's act. Keep all disambiguating context visible.
- **Prerequisites:** Mexican Spanish 01–05 and accepted Stage 06 S01–S04.
- **Exclusions:** All binding stage-wide scope, originality, and advanced-use boundaries; no unrelated adjunct target, repeated formula expanded with arbitrary content, or same/later-slice use assumed as support.
- **Advance beyond Stage 05:** C1 already retracts selectively and preserves unaffected grounds. The new task tracks consequential changes in attribution or support as a corrected source and its derivative accounts diverge; a new revision verb or a second unchanged fact is not progression.

- **Communicative function:** Mediate a working description without forcing either source to accept the other's definition.

## Slice 07 — Conditional guarantees with interacting assumptions

- **IDs:** `es-06-0151`–`es-06-0175` (25 accepted cards).
- **Grammar and construction burden:** Compare the actual reach of two conditional guarantees whose assumptions interact, and produce a focused response that constrains the proposed joint inference.
- **Communicative function:** Clarify what the competing assurances jointly establish without treating a condition for one option as a guarantee for the combined plan.
- **Lexical domains and reusable chunks:** Venue access, project feasibility, shared responsibilities. Select distinct context-licensed forms or indivisible collocations, never an inventory of interchangeable expressions.
- **Pronunciation and orthography burden:** Standard Mexican stress, connected clause grouping, contrastive focus and parenthetical boundaries; canonical NFC accents and punctuation. Text must establish distinctions that prosody alone cannot guarantee.
- **Register and relationship:** Contemporary educated Mexican conversation, neutral explanation, or accessible professional/public communication as the named participants and situation require; preserve relationship-appropriate address.
- **Listening and production targets:** Recover the supplied source, scope, and interaction relations from the complete cue and produce the one focused span that directly performs this slice's act. Keep all disambiguating context visible.
- **Prerequisites:** Mexican Spanish 01–05 and accepted Stage 06 S01–S06.
- **Exclusions:** All binding stage-wide scope, originality, and advanced-use boundaries; no unrelated adjunct target, repeated formula expanded with arbitrary content, or same/later-slice use assumed as support.
- **Advance beyond Stage 05:** C1 already identifies necessity, sufficiency, missing links, incompatible resources, and unsupported chains. The new task responds to the interaction between two stated justifications: preserve what each establishes under its own assumptions while correcting how they are combined. A new example of one inherited fallacy is not progression.

## Slice 08 — Revising objections after an interacting remedy

- **IDs:** `es-06-0176`–`es-06-0200` (25 accepted cards).
- **Grammar and construction burden:** Respond to a proposed remedy whose effect on one objection changes the force or relevance of another stated ground, and revise only the affected part of the recommendation.
- **Communicative function:** Acknowledge the remedy while negotiating a revised recommendation that reflects the interaction, not merely two independent pros and cons.
- **Lexical domains and reusable chunks:** Workloads, service recovery, housing and travel plans. Select distinct context-licensed forms or indivisible collocations, never an inventory of interchangeable expressions.
- **Pronunciation and orthography burden:** Standard Mexican stress, connected clause grouping, contrastive focus and parenthetical boundaries; canonical NFC accents and punctuation. Text must establish distinctions that prosody alone cannot guarantee.
- **Register and relationship:** Contemporary educated Mexican conversation, neutral explanation, or accessible professional/public communication as the named participants and situation require; preserve relationship-appropriate address.
- **Listening and production targets:** Recover the supplied source, scope, and interaction relations from the complete cue and produce the one focused span that directly performs this slice's act. Keep all disambiguating context visible.
- **Prerequisites:** Mexican Spanish 01–05 and accepted Stage 06 S01–S07.
- **Exclusions:** All binding stage-wide scope, originality, and advanced-use boundaries; no unrelated adjunct target, repeated formula expanded with arbitrary content, or same/later-slice use assumed as support.
- **Advance beyond Stage 05:** C1 already recognizes partial mitigation, unaffected objections, tradeoffs and separable decisions. Here a remedy changes how two stated grounds support or oppose the same recommendation; the response must preserve their revised relationship rather than list a remaining drawback or rename a criticism.

## Slice 09 — Answering the strongest objection across competing accounts

- **IDs:** `es-06-0201`–`es-06-0225` (25 accepted cards).
- **Grammar and construction burden:** Repair an ally or intermediary's consequential distortion of a stated objection and answer the strongest supported version without inventing an opponent's claim.
- **Communicative function:** Preserve the original objection's force and source while responding to the point actually contested, not a convenient weaker or stronger substitute.
- **Lexical domains and reusable chunks:** Project proposals, family decisions, public discussion. Select distinct context-licensed forms or indivisible collocations, never an inventory of interchangeable expressions.
- **Pronunciation and orthography burden:** Standard Mexican stress, connected clause grouping, contrastive focus and parenthetical boundaries; canonical NFC accents and punctuation. Text must establish distinctions that prosody alone cannot guarantee.
- **Register and relationship:** Contemporary educated Mexican conversation, neutral explanation, or accessible professional/public communication as the named participants and situation require; preserve relationship-appropriate address.
- **Listening and production targets:** Recover the supplied source, scope, and interaction relations from the complete cue and produce the one focused span that directly performs this slice's act. Keep all disambiguating context visible.
- **Prerequisites:** Mexican Spanish 01–05 and accepted Stage 06 S01–S08.
- **Exclusions:** All binding stage-wide scope, originality, and advanced-use boundaries; no unrelated adjunct target, repeated formula expanded with arbitrary content, or same/later-slice use assumed as support.
- **Advance beyond Stage 05:** C1 already distinguishes valid objections, irrelevant replies, flawed analogies and excessive demands. The new task navigates competing representations of the same objection: correct the consequential shift introduced by another participant while preserving a responsive argument about the original claim.

## Slice 10 — Reciprocal reach of a concession in competing arguments

- **IDs:** `es-06-0226`–`es-06-0250` (25 accepted cards).
- **Grammar and construction burden:** Make a bounded retraction whose discredited premise also supports part of a competing argument, applying the correction consistently while preserving each side's independent grounds.
- **Communicative function:** Concede what the evidence defeats without granting wholesale capitulation or allowing the same defeated premise to survive selectively in a rival conclusion.
- **Lexical domains and reusable chunks:** Disputed accounts, feedback, recommendations. Select distinct context-licensed forms or indivisible collocations, never an inventory of interchangeable expressions.
- **Pronunciation and orthography burden:** Standard Mexican stress, connected clause grouping, contrastive focus and parenthetical boundaries; canonical NFC accents and punctuation. Text must establish distinctions that prosody alone cannot guarantee.
- **Register and relationship:** Contemporary educated Mexican conversation, neutral explanation, or accessible professional/public communication as the named participants and situation require; preserve relationship-appropriate address.
- **Listening and production targets:** Recover the supplied source, scope, and interaction relations from the complete cue and produce the one focused span that directly performs this slice's act. Keep all disambiguating context visible.
- **Prerequisites:** Mexican Spanish 01–05 and accepted Stage 06 S01–S09.
- **Exclusions:** All binding stage-wide scope, originality, and advanced-use boundaries; no unrelated adjunct target, repeated formula expanded with arbitrary content, or same/later-slice use assumed as support.
- **Advance beyond Stage 05:** C1 already retracts selectively, preserves an independent recommendation, rejects double standards and distinguishes a failed argument from a false conclusion. The new response tracks the reciprocal consequences of one conceded premise across competing arguments, while retaining separately supported grounds on either side; it is not another retraction verb or retelling correction.

## Slice 11 — Counterexamples across interacting qualifications

- **IDs:** `es-06-0251`–`es-06-0275` (25 accepted cards).
- **Grammar and construction burden:** Respond to a counterexample whose relevance differs across interacting qualifications of the stated claim, preserving both a legitimate exception and the objection to the aspect it does not exempt.
- **Communicative function:** Limit the protective reach of an exception without treating an excluded case as a refutation of everything or dismissing its independently relevant objection.
- **Lexical domains and reusable chunks:** Education, cultural interpretation, accessibility, service comparison. Select distinct context-licensed forms or indivisible collocations, never an inventory of interchangeable expressions.
- **Pronunciation and orthography burden:** Standard Mexican stress, connected clause grouping, contrastive focus and parenthetical boundaries; canonical NFC accents and punctuation. Text must establish distinctions that prosody alone cannot guarantee.
- **Register and relationship:** Contemporary educated Mexican conversation, neutral explanation, or accessible professional/public communication as the named participants and situation require; preserve relationship-appropriate address.
- **Listening and production targets:** Recover the supplied source, scope, and interaction relations from the complete cue and produce the one focused span that directly performs this slice's act. Keep all disambiguating context visible.
- **Prerequisites:** Mexican Spanish 01–05 and accepted Stage 06 S01–S10.
- **Exclusions:** All binding stage-wide scope, originality, and advanced-use boundaries; no unrelated adjunct target, repeated formula expanded with arbitrary content, or same/later-slice use assumed as support.
- **Advance beyond Stage 05:** C1 already tests out-of-domain counterexamples, changed criteria and universal-versus-qualified revisions. The new response distinguishes what an explicit exception actually protects from a separate still-applicable commitment within the same contested claim; an added exception label or a new example alone is not progression.

## Slice 12 — Negotiating what a shared trial can establish

- **IDs:** `es-06-0276`–`es-06-0300` (25 accepted cards).
- **Grammar and construction burden:** Negotiate a bounded evidential criterion when participants propose different conclusions from the same trial, matching each stated observation to the decision it can support without inventing a shared standard.
- **Communicative function:** Agree on a usable test and limited next step while preserving which competing decision criteria or conclusions remain unresolved.
- **Lexical domains and reusable chunks:** Community trials, event logistics, ordinary purchases. Select distinct context-licensed forms or indivisible collocations, never an inventory of interchangeable expressions.
- **Pronunciation and orthography burden:** Standard Mexican stress, connected clause grouping, contrastive focus and parenthetical boundaries; canonical NFC accents and punctuation. Text must establish distinctions that prosody alone cannot guarantee.
- **Register and relationship:** Contemporary educated Mexican conversation, neutral explanation, or accessible professional/public communication as the named participants and situation require; preserve relationship-appropriate address.
- **Listening and production targets:** Recover the supplied source, scope, and interaction relations from the complete cue and produce the one focused span that directly performs this slice's act. Keep all disambiguating context visible.
- **Prerequisites:** Mexican Spanish 01–05 and accepted Stage 06 S01–S11.
- **Exclusions:** All binding stage-wide scope, originality, and advanced-use boundaries; no unrelated adjunct target, repeated formula expanded with arbitrary content, or same/later-slice use assumed as support.
- **Advance beyond Stage 05:** C1 already supports trials without guaranteeing success, asks what evidence would change an objection, and changes stopping criteria. The new task negotiates the relation between different participants' evidential questions about the same trial and the separate decisions those answers could justify; it is not a new provisional-approval formula.

## Slice 13 — Commitment under contested nested attribution

- **IDs:** `es-06-0301`–`es-06-0325` (25 accepted cards).
- **Grammar and construction burden:** Produce a focused stance or modal span that preserves which source commits to a proposition when a nested account is quoted, challenged or denied; distinguish denial of an attribution from denial of its content.
- **Communicative function:** Take a warranted position without transferring one reporting level's endorsement, doubt or denial to another.
- **Lexical domains and reusable chunks:** Workplace messages, neighborhood reports, family accounts. Select distinct context-licensed forms or indivisible collocations, never an inventory of interchangeable expressions.
- **Pronunciation and orthography burden:** Standard Mexican stress, connected clause grouping, contrastive focus and parenthetical boundaries; canonical NFC accents and punctuation. Text must establish distinctions that prosody alone cannot guarantee.
- **Register and relationship:** Contemporary educated Mexican conversation, neutral explanation, or accessible professional/public communication as the named participants and situation require; preserve relationship-appropriate address.
- **Listening and production targets:** Recover the supplied source, scope, and interaction relations from the complete cue and produce the one focused span that directly performs this slice's act. Keep all disambiguating context visible.
- **Prerequisites:** Mexican Spanish 01–05 and accepted Stage 06 S01–S12.
- **Exclusions:** All binding stage-wide scope, originality, and advanced-use boundaries; no unrelated adjunct target, repeated formula expanded with arbitrary content, or same/later-slice use assumed as support.
- **Advance beyond Stage 05:** C1 already separates report, personal endorsement, source reliability and limited corroboration. The new task calibrates commitment when a challenge at one reporting level changes what another speaker may claim about that account without settling its underlying content; an extra reporter or a rarer evidential verb alone is not progression.

## Slice 14 — Factual concessions across competing explanations

- **IDs:** `es-06-0326`–`es-06-0350` (25 accepted cards).
- **Grammar and construction burden:** Concede a shared observation while distinguishing the force it has under two explicitly competing explanations, and express one's own limited causal commitment without borrowing another participant's certainty.
- **Communicative function:** Preserve a useful factual agreement without turning it into agreement on why the result occurred or which alternative explanation has been ruled out.
- **Lexical domains and reusable chunks:** Project delays, institutional communication, cultural review. Select distinct context-licensed forms or indivisible collocations, never an inventory of interchangeable expressions.
- **Pronunciation and orthography burden:** Standard Mexican stress, connected clause grouping, contrastive focus and parenthetical boundaries; canonical NFC accents and punctuation. Text must establish distinctions that prosody alone cannot guarantee.
- **Register and relationship:** Contemporary educated Mexican conversation, neutral explanation, or accessible professional/public communication as the named participants and situation require; preserve relationship-appropriate address.
- **Listening and production targets:** Recover the supplied source, scope, and interaction relations from the complete cue and produce the one focused span that directly performs this slice's act. Keep all disambiguating context visible.
- **Prerequisites:** Mexican Spanish 01–05 and accepted Stage 06 S01–S13.
- **Exclusions:** All binding stage-wide scope, originality, and advanced-use boundaries; no unrelated adjunct target, repeated formula expanded with arbitrary content, or same/later-slice use assumed as support.
- **Advance beyond Stage 05:** C1 already concedes facts without endorsing an attached explanation. The supplied difference must affect the actual response, not merely add another source or a causal synonym.

The new cross-medium task decides what an accountable summary can assert about an experience narrated from within. The focal span must perform that transformation, not merely supply a new reporting verb.

The new task changes audience and medium: preserve an effect whose original wording would mislead outside its shared context, without pretending an inferred intention is proven. No technical diagnosis, advice, interface-field inventory or mere synonym for a C1 distinction.

Here disagreement about the consultation changes how the substantive responses may represent their sources. Address the interacting objection and representation problem, not another fairness label, voting detail or silence-as-consent example.

The added task recognizes a limited supplied regional contrast whose interpretation changes the current interaction. Mix address, lexical reference, event identity and pragmatic uptake across distinct acts; no regional-equivalent dictionary, conjugation table or repeated clarification template.
