| `es-06-0739` | `Autorizarías` | Request a limited identification visit that lets the parties establish the repair object together before deciding on the repair. | Unlike simply reopening a rejected repair, negotiate a separate preparatory step whose acceptance does not authorize the work or retract the refusal about the entrance. |
| `es-06-0740` | `cotejemos` | Compare the parties' understood loan terms after discovering that two object descriptions refer to one table. | Use identity clarification to expose an unresolved condition on moving the object, rather than assuming that agreement to clear a surface grants relocation permission. |
| `es-06-0741` | `se lo consulte` | Ask an intermediary to put an accommodation request to the host with the intended guest explicitly identified. | Beyond representing another person's needs, request a fresh hosting decision without transferring an offer understood as being for a partner to an interpreter. |

| `es-06-0747` | `pregúntaselo` | Request an explicit participant change after a shared-account reply came from someone other than the intended invitee. | Beyond identifying the writer or recipient of a message, seek a new seat-allocation decision without transferring an invitation through account access. |

| `es-06-0749` | `nos confirmen` | Seek joint confirmation of the event boundary that determines how long a transport offer lasts. | Beyond negotiating a waiting time, repair agreement around differently identified events before the mediator relays a transport commitment. |
| `es-06-0750` | `rectifícalo` | Require correction of the mediator's own relay before an unrecognized relationship expands a personal loan into business use. | Beyond preserving source identification, repair an imminent practical commitment created by the mediator's substitution and leave the new use subject to a separate decision. |
| `es-06-0751` | `descartabas` | Clarify whether a negative reply rejects the interrupter's no-contact alternative while preserving the mother's reservation about obligatory calls. | The repair separates rejection of a newly imposed alternative from acceptance of the original offer; clarifying the negative reply cannot erase the concern that prompted the interruption. |

| `es-06-0800` | `se oponía` | Restore the proposal under discussion as the object of an elliptical refusal while retaining the existing venue arrangement. | The destination lacks the shared question about an additional use; reformulation attaches the refusal to that addition instead of turning it into cancellation of the previously reserved venue. |

| `es-06-0805` | `calificarla` | Reject an unsupported judgment based on a protected reason's absence from the briefing. | Withholding the explanation limits the audience's evaluation; it does not establish that the request lacks a reason. |

| `es-06-0885` | `le garantizo` | Preserve a tentative estimate when the speaker is now addressing a customer as staff. | The destination explicitly withholds a service guarantee that the same estimate could acquire at the counter. |
| `es-06-0886` | `emitiré` | Carry sympathy into an organizational response while declining a requested judgment unsupported by the source. | The liaison refuses to turn a friend's sympathetic response into the institutional condemnation now being requested. |
| `es-06-0887` | `Convénceme` | Transform a peer objection into an invitation to persuade the person who now decides. | The destination gives the photographer an opportunity to influence the pending decision rather than merely relabeling the objection as personal. |
| `es-06-0888` | `Le consulto` | Turn a former decision maker's proposal into a request to the person now responsible. | The destination requests a room decision instead of carrying the speaker's former authority into the new participant role. |
| `es-06-0889` | `Me orienta` | Recast a peer's learning question as a request for guidance from a supervisor. | The destination places the supervisor in the learner's position so the question does not act as a demand to defend an alleged error. |
| `es-06-0890` | `te deja fuera` | Restate freedom from required attendance as a relative without suggesting exclusion. | The destination preserves the participant's place and welcomes his company without creating an exemption or restoring an attendance duty. |
| `es-06-0891` | `ofrezco una disculpa` | Carry a personal apology into a staff response to a complaint involving several people. | The destination answers for the speaker's own lapse without letting the workplace channel imply an explanation of everyone else's conduct. |
| `es-06-0892` | `Me autorizas` | Ask for permission to use a friend's anecdote after acquiring control over presentation assignments. | The destination makes consent to using the anecdote independent of the presentation slot the coordinator has already confirmed. |
| `es-06-0893` | `te representaré` | Refuse a representative mandate that exceeds the agreement previously expressed as a family confidant. | The destination declines to act for the aunt's requested remedy; it does more than repeat C1's bounded agreement. |
| `es-06-0894` | `Notifico` | Convert a private announcement of withdrawal into notice from an organizer. | The destination withdraws the speaker from presenting without canceling the event or turning possible continuation into a confirmed plan. |
| `es-06-0895` | `Responderé` | Restate a personal undertaking from the team's contact without transferring it to the team. | The destination commits the speaker personally where the new representative role could diffuse responsibility or imply a collective promise. |
| `es-06-0896` | `darme gusto` | Preserve delegated choice when siblings turn the decision into an attempt to please the coordinator personally. | The destination removes a family expectation of pleasing the speaker without issuing a second delegation or requiring rejection of the speaker's preference. |
| `es-06-0897` | `les daré preferencia` | Reformulate friendly praise while refusing the preferential treatment requested from a new judge. | The destination preserves praise for the scene while denying that the friendship will affect comparative judging. |
| `es-06-0898` | `Te recuerdo` | Carry a shared deadline into an intimate conversation without making it a personal demand. | The reminder invokes the existing workshop agreement despite the changed relationship, rather than seeking special compliance for the speaker. |
| `es-06-0899` | `Tomen en cuenta` | Turn a peer warning into a coordinator's request to weigh a risk without prescribing a program change. | The destination directs consideration of the source's uncertain consequence while leaving the resulting scheduling decision with the team. |
| `es-06-0900` | `Rehúso` | Turn a personal refusal into an organizer's response without prohibiting participation by others. | The destination refuses inclusion of the speaker while leaving other participants' choices and the speaker's separate help intact. |
| `es-06-0901` | `Me ciño` | Bound an accountable explanation to the incident questioned before the relay generalized it. | Answers the original timing challenge with a cause and personal responsibility while refusing the relay's habitual scope. |
| `es-06-0902` | `Puedo ofrecer` | Answer a relayed contingency question with a conditional offer rather than an invented prior promise. | Supplies the requested fallback while keeping its condition and distinguishing a present offer from an alleged commitment. |
| `es-06-0903` | `Contrasto` | Address a reported interpretive objection without assigning it to the person who asked about it. | Provides a textual answer to the borrowed objection while repairing the relay's transfer of ownership. |
| `es-06-0904` | `Los eximo` | Waive an unnecessary submission requirement after a question about its necessity is relayed as refusal. | Acts on the participants' substantive challenge by removing the unsupported requirement instead of treating them as disobedient. |
| `es-06-0905` | `Sustento` | Supply bounded grounds for a proposal after the relay upgrades the question to proof of universal preference. | Distinguishes the evidence owed for a trial proposal from the stronger proof demanded only by the relayer. |
| `es-06-0906` | `Les pido disculpas` | Apologize for an established omission without accepting a malicious motive added in transmission. | Performs the response owed to the affected audience while separating actual responsibility from the relay's allegation of intent. |
| `es-06-0907` | `Rectificaría` | Answer willingness to revise under a condition without turning it into an overdue unconditional promise. | Restores the original contingency and gives the requested conditional commitment while rejecting invented ownership of a demand. |
| `es-06-0908` | `hago extensiva` | Extend participation to the excluded subgroup without adopting a relayed demand to cancel everyone else's session. | Answers the original access challenge through an additional opportunity while restoring the scope lost in the relay. |
| `es-06-0909` | `les facilito` | Give the requested route for an access request instead of assigning blame for an invented promise. | Supplies the practical response owed by the course contact while preserving the difference between requesting access and reporting a breach. |
| `es-06-0910` | `Comprobaré` | Undertake the verification requested without using the questioner as a witness supplied by the relay. | Turns the original evidential challenge into a concrete checking procedure while withholding the eyewitness status added in transmission. |
| `es-06-0911` | `Le debo` | Discharge the obligation to explain both a reassignment and its missing notice after the relay presents it as exclusion. | Answers the participant's actual two-part concern with a reason and responsibility while correcting the alleged removal from the activity. |
| `es-06-0912` | `Demostraré` | Demonstrate a requested fallback procedure without defending a general policy invented in the relay. | Answers the original how-to challenge with a worked contingency while preserving the difference between fallback competence and policy endorsement. |
| `es-06-0913` | `les doy a elegir` | Restore the group's choice when a challenge to unilateral selection is relayed as an already settled collective preference. | Explains the initial selection and opens the decision to the group instead of implementing a preference the relayer invented. |
| `es-06-0914` | `reservo` | Withhold identifying information as an answer to a privacy challenge relayed as a ban on the entire account. | Performs the limited protection originally requested while preserving the possibility of telling the substantive anecdote. |
| `es-06-0915` | `Preciso` | Specify the outstanding information after a bounded question is relayed as global uncertainty. | Answers what remains unknown while preventing the relayer's broader doubt from becoming the questioner's position. |
| `es-06-0916` | `someto a prueba` | Test the guide against the original novice-use challenge instead of defending it against an attributed rejection. | Produces a bounded evaluative answer from an explicit trial while preserving the questioner's unsettled stance. |
| `es-06-0917` | `le planteo` | Put an alternative interpretation to the invited speaker for examination after the relay turns a request for debate into a demand to exclude him. | Answers how the competing reading will be examined by challenging the guest publicly, preserving his participation without merely adding another speaker. |
| `es-06-0918` | `quedo a deber` | Acknowledge an unanswered causal question without reframing it as a personal accusation to dismiss. | Preserves the original explanatory debt and identifies the missing evidence after the relay changes the question into an allegation. |
| `es-06-0919` | `Distingo` | Separate the historical proposal from its current version when answering a relayed challenge to an allegedly continuing stance. | Explains the original past objection and its resolved basis without accepting the relay's extension into the present. |
| `es-06-0920` | `estoy dispuesto` | Accept a voluntary communication request without accepting a stronger demand assigned to its beneficiary. | Gives a usable affirmative response to the original access concern while distinguishing willingness from an invented obligation. |
| `es-06-0921` | `Separo` | Answer both parts of a mixed evaluation after the relay replaces them with a global failure claim. | Preserves the original two-part response obligation and gives distinct positive and negative findings without assigning the verdict to its questioner. |
| `es-06-0922` | `sopesaré` | Weigh a hypothetical change instead of defending a preference the relay assigns to the group. | Provides the requested tradeoff analysis while preserving its role before a decision rather than after an alleged endorsement. |
| `es-06-0923` | `canalizo` | Route reported complaints for case review without treating their messenger as the affected witness. | Answers the original procedural challenge while preserving whose experience can supply the details needed for that review. |
| `es-06-0924` | `Aporto` | Answer a request for the basis of a memory with documentary support limited to the event's planned month. | Supplies the evidence the niece requested without defending every remembered detail against the relayer's invented fabrication charge or treating a planned date as proof of occurrence. |
| `es-06-0925` | `Retomaré` | Restore unanswered online questions to the response sequence after a challenge about their omission is relayed as withdrawal. | Undertakes the still-owed answers and allows follow-up after reopening the chat, rather than treating the audience as having abandoned its questions. |
| `es-06-0926` | `avalé` | Limit endorsement of corrected wording to its description of a proposal. | Repairs the use of a reply approving a correction as new evidence of assent at the meeting. |
| `es-06-0927` | `se incorporó` | Locate the adoption of a venue in the exchange prompted by the summary. | Preserves a valid later agreement while correcting the meeting history created from its acceptance replies. |
| `es-06-0928` | `se presume` | Reject presumed earlier approval based on silence during record review. | Separates the summary's later invitation to correct errors from the consultation explicitly left pending at the meeting. |
| `es-06-0929` | `atestigua` | Reject a recipient's derivative response as testimony to an earlier decision. | Identifies the circular support created when a reader's understanding of the circulated record becomes evidence for that record. |
| `es-06-0930` | `rectifiquen` | Repair a rewritten record that turns thanks for writing into acceptance of an assignment. | Recovers the force of a later response after selective reporting of that response manufactures an earlier appointment. |
| `es-06-0931` | `Separemos` | Separate correction of a nonexistent charge from adoption of a later funding proposal. | Keeps two acts generated by circulation from being merged into one supposed meeting decision. |
| `es-06-0932` | `Reabran` | Reopen discussion of an unresolved rehearsal falsely recorded as confirmed through conditional attendance replies. | Applies the unmet condition to the status of the circulated meeting record rather than treating replies as retrospective joint confirmation. |
| `es-06-0933` | `se asentó` | Identify the conversion of later volunteering into a recorded appointment. | Repairs the decision-making agency invented when the summary's own request for volunteers is folded into meeting history. |
| `es-06-0934` | `se añadió` | Remove the decision status assigned to an unanswered addition while preserving the original agreement. | Tracks how a suggestion made during circulation expands an otherwise valid meeting decision. |
| `es-06-0935` | `se le adjudique` | Prevent a later alternative from being attributed as earlier support for cancellation. | Restores a participant's original stance after his response to the circulated account is used to rewrite it. |
| `es-06-0936` | `Me adherí` | Acknowledge present support without erasing the earlier opposition resolved during circulation. | Preserves an actual change of stance caused by a response to the summary instead of projecting current assent backward. |
| `es-06-0937` | `Actualicemos` | Update the operative plan while retaining the decision it later replaced. | Distinguishes changing an accepted decision through circulation from rewriting what the meeting originally decided. |
| `es-06-0938` | `Reenvíen` | Send a privately raised correction to recipients whose responses to the uncorrected summary are being used against it. | Repairs unequal access to a correction before circulation-generated responses are treated as confirmation of meeting history. |
| `es-06-0939` | `sustituyeron` | Identify the replacement of an original conditional commitment with an unconditional promise after its condition is met. | Preserves the practical effect of confirmation obtained through circulation without projecting the newly fulfilled condition back into the original commitment. |
| `es-06-0940` | `habíamos autorizado` | Deny prior publication permission while preserving the limited later selection act. | Recovers an unresolved authorization after the summary steers recipients into a task whose completion is misread as earlier consent. |
| `es-06-0941` | `Habríamos aprobado` | Recover the counterfactual force of approval elicited by a follow-up question. | Prevents a hypothetical reply embedded in record circulation from replacing an actual negative meeting decision. |
| `es-06-0942` | `me retracté` | Reject the recording of an error correction as withdrawal of prior support. | Repairs a second distortion created by revising the summary in response to a denial of its original claim. |
| `es-06-0943` | `Negocié` | Locate a first agreement in later negotiation rather than treat it as a reduction of an earlier promise. | Preserves the new agreement reached in response to circulation without accepting the commitment invented by the summary. |
| `es-06-0944` | `corríjanla` | Restore a meeting's majority result after replies from absent members are added to its vote count. | Prevents preferences elicited by circulation from changing the electorate and result attributed to the earlier meeting. |
| `es-06-0945` | `desvincúlenlas` | Detach scoped replies from the recording permission incorrectly attached to them. | Repairs the shared record when responses supporting one agreed activity are used to validate an addition made during circulation. |
| `es-06-0946` | `Eliminen` | Remove a false cancellation agreement while acknowledging the actual reservation canceled in response to the summary. | Distinguishes an action caused by a false notice from evidence for that notice, without assuming record correction restores the lost meeting space. |
| `es-06-0947` | `Documentemos` | Use original interventions to establish the record instead of treating summary-led recollections as independent evidence. | Addresses apparent corroboration produced when attendees adopt the circulated account as their own reconstruction. |
| `es-06-0948` | `se originó` | Trace apparent consensus to replies premised on the summary's claim that consensus already existed. | Exposes the feedback through which a circulated claim of agreement elicits conditional support later used to confirm the same claim. |
| `es-06-0949` | `Desautoricé` | Reject an unauthorized allocation without withdrawing the original limited offer. | Prevents a reply disputing the summary's expansion from being used to erase the actual earlier commitment. |
| `es-06-0950` | `me opuse` | Keep rejection of a circulated account from becoming a recorded vote against its proposal. | Repairs retrospective dissent manufactured from a response challenging the summary while leaving the participant's decision open. |
| `es-06-0951` | `Desglosemos` | Separate shared wording edits from a revision that changes where accepted practice takes place. | The requested shortening affects an unshared change from classroom practice to homework, requiring a distinct instructional decision. |
| `es-06-0952` | `reordenemos` | Propose a shared sequence that makes shortening compatible with differently accepted teaching arrangements. | Combines an unseen sequencing change with its effect on who receives instruction before practicing. |
| `es-06-0953` | `me encargaría` | Offer bounded responsibility for cuts that now require changes to an added deliverable. | Negotiates the interaction between an unreceived subtitling requirement and a new editing request. |
| `es-06-0954` | `Anonimicemos` | Propose anonymization before shortening material whose accepted audience has changed. | The public version changes which details a brevity edit can preserve, while the contributor knows only the family version. |
| `es-06-0955` | `reintegremos` | Restore a removed access component as a separate part while negotiating a shorter audio delivery. | Uses mismatched accepted formats to preserve written access without transferring text production to the audio contributor. |
| `es-06-0956` | `reabriría` | Reopen the evaluation criterion affected by an unshared change in the unit of participation. | A requested simplification now depends on reconciling individual assessment with an accepted collective exercise. |
| `es-06-0957` | `recortaría` | Make a presentation cut conditional on retaining discussion before a newly added audience vote. | Negotiates time across discussion and voting formats accepted by different participants, with the author still unaware of the replacement. |
| `es-06-0958` | `compatibilicemos` | Propose converging route endings while making the extra stop, return and waiting arrangement subject to renewed agreement. | A common departure requires changing the colleague's accepted endpoint and consulting the participant who has not received that route. |
| `es-06-0959` | `recomponemos` | Seek a menu revision that reduces tableware by combining courses as an alternative to an unshared self-service plan. | Negotiates the relation between meal composition and serving format across different acceptances, without treating reduced tableware as acceptance of self-service. |
| `es-06-0960` | `alternemos` | Propose alternating short passages as a negotiated alternative to an unshared role replacement. | Shortening is negotiated together with a changed speaking role, rather than inferred from either earlier acceptance. |
| `es-06-0961` | `acotaría` | Offer language editing of newly added assessment questions with substantive validation assigned separately. | Revises the reviewer's undertaking across an unreceived change from explanatory to evaluative content, making the expanded offer conditional on a check of assessment meaning. |
| `es-06-0962` | `suprimiría` | Counter a shortening request that would remove the choice to decline a newly added recurring commitment. | Revises default enrollment across unshared accepted scopes while exposing the effect of optional participation on the workshop leader's plan. |
| `es-06-0963` | `reemplazaríamos` | Offer shorter descriptions as a jointly negotiable alternative to an unshared removal of illustrations. | Connects the requested type enlargement to different accepted layouts and identifies the compromise both parties must assess. |
| `es-06-0964` | `revisaríamos` | Propose a joint image-and-caption review before shortening text against differently accepted visual contents. | The caption edit depends on an unseen crop and requires decisions from both the original approver and the projection approver. |
| `es-06-0965` | `reorganizaríamos` | Negotiate a material-saving group arrangement between individual practice and an unshared demonstration-only plan. | The requested reduction changes participation differently under each accepted format and needs a concrete intermediate arrangement. |
| `es-06-0966` | `intercambiemos` | Propose a temporary role swap so each participant can be photographed interviewing, subject to revising continuous recording. | The separate portraits require changing an unshared video commitment and the interviewer's role, with both participants deciding on those consequences. |
| `es-06-0967` | `retrabajemos` | Negotiate a transition rewrite whose reference depends on differently accepted route orders. | An unshared relocation changes the truth of the retained wording, so shortening requires a shared sequencing decision. |
| `es-06-0968` | `retomaría` | Propose restoring an omitted knowledge limit as the basis for revising an overconfident ending. | The requested tone change is traced to an unshared deletion, requiring reconciliation of the two accepted narrative scopes. |
| `es-06-0969` | `compensaríamos` | Offer a duration tradeoff that restores reading time removed from a separately accepted edit. | Negotiates a compensating music cut because the requested access revision conflicts with an unshared pause-free montage. |
| `es-06-0970` | `simulemos` | Propose a simulation to assess a changed material arrangement before revising practical instructions. | Makes a trial of separate materials inform a shared decision across accepted classroom and online formats, instead of treating the unseen online plan as settled. |
| `es-06-0971` | `adaptemos` | Propose making instructions self-contained before shortening them for an unshared format without spoken explanations. | The explicit removal of personal explanations changes what concise signs must communicate and requires the artisans to decide on the revised format. |
| `es-06-0972` | `uniformemos` | Propose a common confirmation criterion that requires revising an accepted automatic response. | Negotiates the operative trigger concealed by a wording simplification, with separate decisions from reception and the approver who has not seen the form. |
| `es-06-0973` | `reubiquemos` | Negotiate relocating essential explanations instead of deleting links under an unshared layout change. | The requested visual simplification affects access to instructions differently in the two accepted placements. |
| `es-06-0974` | `me sumaría` | Offer conditional participation in a revised format with a concrete arrangement for ongoing orientation. | The proposed deletion becomes consequential under an unshared shift from a fixed group to continuous arrivals. |
| `es-06-0975` | `intercalaría` | Offer an interleaved abridgment that preserves contributions replaced in a separately accepted script. | Negotiates a combined script requiring the guide to assess added testimony and the neighbor to assess shorter memories, rather than transferring either acceptance. |
| `es-06-0976` | `se la adjudicas` | Reject obtaining a new promise as the remedy for a guarantee introduced by the presenter. | The feedback separates correcting what the team was told from negotiating a commitment the source has not made. |
| `es-06-0977` | `la suscribes` | Reject softening a source claim as a way to repair apparent editorial endorsement. | The repair preserves an objectionable source position while distinguishing it from the writer's criticism; attribution cannot be repaired by changing the view attributed. |
| `es-06-0978` | `le cargas` | Repair misplaced decision-making responsibility without certifying the messenger's account. | Correcting the agent also requires preserving the source of the attribution, so the audience does not receive an unverified replacement accusation as fact. |
| `es-06-0979` | `lo haces extensivo` | Reject deleting a contributor entirely to repair an overextended endorsement. | The feedback preserves usable support and the same source's opposition while correcting the public role created by the summary. |
| `es-06-0980` | `prolongas` | Explain why dating a source does not repair a present-tense intention created by its adaptation. | The feedback distinguishes the date of the remembered account from the current commitment conveyed by the editor's wording. |
| `es-06-0981` | `se desdiga` | Reject a demand that the source retract an accusation introduced by the video. | The repair addresses audience fallout without making the original speaker own the reformulator's statement. |
| `es-06-0982` | `universalizas` | Reject source approval as a remedy for a testimonial generalization introduced by the editor. | The feedback separates permission to use wording from the evidence it supplies to prospective participants. |
| `es-06-0983` | `respaldo expreso` | Distinguish a newly obtained endorsement from the non-objection misreported earlier. | Later agreement changes what may now be announced but does not make the earlier transformation faithful. |
| `es-06-0984` | `falta de voluntad` | Reject persuasion as a response to unwillingness invented in an access report. | The feedback connects the adaptation's false characterization to an unsuitable response and restores a communication-focused remedy. |
| `es-06-0985` | `Le pones en boca` | Reject adding a source label that would reinforce the assignment of a rejected opinion to its reporter. | The feedback repairs both opinion ownership and the reporter's stance; an added attribution alone would deepen the misrepresentation. |
| `es-06-0986` | `corre por tu cuenta` | Reject transferring an editor's conjectural motive to the source merely by hedging it. | The feedback distinguishes uncertainty about an editorial interpretation from uncertainty expressed by the original participant. |
| `es-06-0987` | `te compromete` | Reject an impersonal rewrite that hides responsibility instead of correcting a copied promise. | The feedback removes an apparent undertaking by the new sender while keeping the original offer attributable and usable. |
| `es-06-0988` | `avalarla` | Redirect criticism toward the actual source claim while removing an invented prohibition. | The repair retains grounds for disagreement without treating faithful reporting as endorsement or making the source defend the editor's stronger claim. |
| `es-06-0989` | `te desentiendas` | Keep editorial responsibility for an unsupported causal accusation even when the addition is visibly marked. | Feedback distinguishes marking an intervention from justifying it and preserves the source's narrower observation. |
| `es-06-0990` | `Haces pasar` | Reject replacing an invented requirement with a recommendation as the repair for an optional invitation. | The feedback restores the source's invitation without adding a weaker directive and prevents requests for exceptions to an invented requirement. |
| `es-06-0991` | `Inviertes` | Restore the causal direction of a source's explanation without independently certifying it. | Feedback corrects the adaptation's redistribution of blame while keeping the source's causal account attributed. |
| `es-06-0992` | `se arrepintió` | Reject an update that would turn an editor's invented choice into the source's regret. | The feedback distinguishes correction of a report from a change in the source's position, preserving responsibility for the original false announcement. |
| `es-06-0993` | `lo exculpas` | Reject a supposedly neutral rewrite that excuses conduct by replacing the source's stated motive. | The feedback distinguishes removing editorial judgment from improving the source's self-presentation through an invented charitable explanation. |
| `es-06-0994` | `se la traslades` | Assign responsibility for an ambiguous pronoun to the reformulation rather than requesting source clarification. | The feedback repairs the reader's referent while protecting a source whose original statement was already clear. |
| `es-06-0995` | `das por admitido` | Remove an admission presupposed by a headline that reformulates a denial. | The reviewer separates the headline's false confession, the source's denial, and the unresolved truth of the event. |
| `es-06-0996` | `La das por retirada` | Reject a verbatim correction that would breach the identity restriction behind a misreported withdrawal. | The feedback repairs the complaint's active status while rejecting a proof-by-quotation remedy that would expose the protected speaker. |
| `es-06-0997` | `valoración personal` | Reject reversing a collective evaluation as the repair for an overgeneralized family response. | The feedback prevents correction in the opposite direction from creating another false shared stance and distinguishes observed laughter from an expressed review. |
| `es-06-0998` | `trato condescendiente` | Reject replacing a group label while retaining the patronizing generalization introduced by an adaptation. | The repair restores the source's optional, bounded offer rather than leaving the same characterization under more neutral vocabulary. |
| `es-06-0999` | `invitación abierta` | Reject exact quotation as sufficient repair when a display still addresses the wrong recipients. | The feedback distinguishes textual accuracy from the invitation performed by the new display and restores the original addressee. |
| `es-06-1000` | `falsa unanimidad` | Correct invented agreement without asking source speakers to harmonize their positions. | The reviewer places the repair obligation on the summary and preserves disagreement for the destination audience. |
| `es-06-1001` | `Reasignemos` | Redirect tutoring hours after correcting demand, while distinguishing support for enrolled pupils from opening places. | Corrected demand changes the useful allocation of hours; separate control of enrollment prevents turning that allocation into a new-group commitment. |
| `es-06-1002` | `restablecería` | Restore the assistance that produced the successful trial rather than treat its results as spare capacity. | Discovery of hidden assistance reverses the staffing recommendation and removes the basis for a linked enrollment increase controlled by someone else. |
| `es-06-1003` | `desactivemos` | Remove an erroneous submission reminder while retaining communication of a pending admission decision. | Recovered receipt evidence warrants changing one message, while reception's unresolved allocation decision makes eliminating all notices inappropriate. |
| `es-06-1004` | `canalizaría` | Redirect a replacement request after new evidence disproves the suspected missing chapter and establishes a delivery error. | The evidence removes the stated basis for replacement and shifts the needed remedy to the delivery owner; the speaker can refer the recovery request while preserving the order. |
| `es-06-1005` | `Reactivemos` | Reopen a transport consultation after recovering evidence of interest in the associated visit. | New attendance evidence warrants asking again but cannot reinstate transport commitments belonging to individual families; the visit itself remains supported. |
| `es-06-1006` | `Recomendaré` | Recommend staged delivery when faster preparation exposes an approval bottleneck. | The observed gain shifts the limiting step to authors' decisions, allowing an earlier partial delivery but preventing a promise to accelerate the entire output. |
| `es-06-1007` | `Desistiría` | Withdraw a content reduction after corrected departure evidence shifts the relevant change to transport. | The correction preserves the full activity and moves the unresolved intervention outside the speaker's control, rather than merely weakening an objection to duration. |
| `es-06-1008` | `Revoquemos` | Revoke an unsupported exclusion while preserving the page limit and photographers' control of cropping. | Corrected print evidence restores the images as usable material, but divided editorial and cropping authority channels the remaining reduction toward selecting complete images. |
| `es-06-1009` | `Prorroguemos` | Extend only the activity whose evidence and available materials survive the originals' return. | Different fidelity results separate reading from color comparison; control over duration does not extend the owners' loan, so only one linked activity can continue. |
| `es-06-1010` | `Redimensionemos` | Rescale a paid weekly program to a proposed repeat trial that preserves the access condition associated with increased attendance. | Corrected attendance supports testing another free event, not inferring demand for occasional paid sessions; separate venue and performer decisions keep the revised proposal from becoming a calendar commitment. |
| `es-06-1011` | `devolvería` | Return ambiguous questions to their authors for clarification instead of assigning the unresolved content decisions to additional data-entry staff. | The review changes a staffing request into a referral for author decisions; the data-entry lead can continue unambiguous work but must return the blocked questions to those who can revise them. |
| `es-06-1012` | `Ratificaría` | Reaffirm an indoor venue offer after new evidence separates its suitability from the failed outdoor rehearsal. | Contrasting results support the speaker's venue commitment, but moving the performance also requires the choir's decision and release of a separately controlled reservation. |
| `es-06-1013` | `Reajustemos` | Jointly revise two schedules after new evidence identifies their users as the same people. | Participant overlap turns two apparently useful options into a conflicting offer; divided scheduling authority requires a coordinated revision before accepting reservations. |
| `es-06-1014` | `concertaría` | Arrange pickup and onward transport to the promised destination before adopting a shorter walking route. | The walking trial supports a local time saving but omits the new return leg; divided route authority requires a transport arrangement and timing check before the original undertaking can be preserved under the change. |
| `es-06-1015` | `Aplazaría` | Postpone audio publication while allowing verified, authorized written quotations to proceed. | A source review settles identity while exposing a medium-specific permission limit; selection authority supports the written output but cannot complete the linked audio release. |
| `es-06-1016` | `Reconduzcamos` | Replace a retraining proposal with a decision session after classifying the unresolved queries. | Evidence of correct execution removes the training rationale; exception authority determines whose decisions must precede the staff's subsequent responses. |
| `es-06-1017` | `Escalonemos` | Stage installation around another activity's release of newly verified shared resources. | The corrected inventory removes the quantity objection but reveals timed dependence on a decision the installer cannot advance; independent preparation remains possible. |
| `es-06-1018` | `Sostendría` | Preserve a successful readability change while returning newly exposed procedural contradictions to their owner. | The improvement produces evidence of a separate content conflict; design authority supports keeping the improvement but cannot settle the instructions required for publication. |
| `es-06-1019` | `Limitemos` | Limit an accelerated-delivery offer to the collection channel that can actually use the time saved. | A verified production gain supports earlier direct collection but cannot move a retailer's delivery calendar, changing the scope of the offer across linked channels. |
| `es-06-1020` | `Retengamos` | Retain suitable paper for illustrations while allowing the tested substitute for text and preserving the illustrator’s decision over separation. | Different trial results warrant mixed materials; using only the new paper in the main volume would require a separately approved illustration booklet that still uses the old paper. |
